CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
China Modern Dairy Holdings Limited (the “Company” or “Modern Dairy”
together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is the leading dairy farming operator
and raw milk producer in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). Modern
Dairy operates 42 dairy farming companies in the PRC with dairy cows over
450,000 heads and annualized milk yield of over 2.59 million tons. The Group
is a leading dairy farming operator and raw milk producer in terms of herd size
in the PRC. The Company initiated the production model of “Zero-distance
Integration of Forage Grass Planting, Cows Breeding and Milk Processing within
Two Hours”, which is the first in the PRC that applies integrated production
model with SGS certification. Modern Dairy’s products have been awarded
the Gold Prize of Monde Selection which is generally regarded as the “Nobel
Prize” in the food industry, for the past ten consecutive years. In recent years,
our business segment has expanded to feed and forage, intelligent e-commerce
platform and dairy cows breeding.

中國現代牧業控股有限公司（ 「本公司」或「現代牧業」，連同其附屬公司「本集
團」），為中華人民共和國（
            「中國」
               ）乳牛畜牧營運商及原料奶生產商的翹楚。現代
牧業於中國經營42家乳牛牧場公司，擁有逾45萬頭乳牛，年化產奶量達259萬噸
以上。按畜群大小計算，本集團是中國乳牛畜牧營運商及原料奶生產商的行業
翹楚。本公司首創「種養加一體化、零距離2小時」的生產模式，是中國首家一體
化生產模式並通過SGS認證的企業。現代牧業的產品於過去十年連續在素有食
品業「諾貝爾獎」之稱的世界食品品質評鑒大會中獲得金獎。近年，我們的業務
板塊已擴展至飼草料､數智平台及乳牛種業。

                                                                                       二零二三年年報 中國現代牧業控股有限公司   15
 MAJOR AWARDS AND ACHIEVEMENTS
 主要獎項及殊榮



     01       Gold Prize of Monde Selection
              世界食品品質評鑒大會金獎
              Modern Dairy was awarded the Gold Prize of Monde Selection for the tenth consecutive year,
              symbolizing that our premium quality branded milk was widely recognized by consumers.
              現代牧業連續10年榮獲世界食品品質評鑒大會金獎，標誌著我們高質量的品牌奶廣泛獲得消費者的青
              睞。




     02      Top 20 Leading Agricultural Industrialization Enterprises in Anhui Province
             《安徽省農業產業化龍頭企業20強》
              Modern Dairy was awarded the title of “Top 20 Leading Agricultural Industrialization Enterprises in
              Anhui Province”, which signified recognition and acknowledgment of the Company’s achievements
              and contributions in the field of agricultural industrialization in Anhui Province, and highlighted the
              Company’s position as one of the leading operators in promoting and advancing agricultural practices
              and technologies in Anhui Province.
              現代牧業獲得《安徽省農業產業化龍頭企業20強》的稱號，意味著現代牧業在安徽省農業產業化領域的成
              就和貢獻得到了認可和肯定，亦突出了本公司在推動和推進安徽省農業實踐和技術方面的領先地位。




     03       BBB Investment Grade Rating Assigned by Standard & Poor’s
              標普授予投資級BBB評級
              Modern Dairy was again assigned a “BBB” investment grade rating by Standard & Poor’s, with a stable
              outlook. Modern Dairy became the first dairy farming company in the world having been assigned an
              international credit rating of investment grade. This fully affirms the recognition received by the Company
              for China’s large-scale farming model from international credit rating agencies. Such grading established
              a positive image for the industry in the international capital market.
              現代牧業再次獲得標普授予投資級BBB評級，評級展望為穩定。現代牧業作為全球首家獲得國際信用評
              級為投資級的奶牛養殖企業。這充分印證了國際信用評級機構對本公司乃至中國規模化養殖模式的肯
              定，為業界在國際資本市場中樹立了良好形象。




     04       Green Development Typical Case
              綠色發展典型案例
              Modern Dairy was awarded the “Green Development Typical Case” from the China Dairy Industry
              Association which recognised outstanding contributions in the field of green development in China.
              現代牧業獲得中國乳製品工業協會「綠色發展典型案例」獎，意味著在我國的綠色發展領域做出了突出貢
              獻。

                                                                    主要獎項及殊榮




05   National Core Breeding Farm For Dairy Cows
     「國家奶牛核心育種場」授牌
     Tongliao farm, one of our dairy farms was awarded the title of “National Core Breeding Farm for Dairy
     Cattle”, which affirms the development direction and achievements of the Group in the breeding
     industry.
     集團旗下通遼牧場獲「國家奶牛核心育種場」授牌，肯定了本集团在種業方面的正确發展方向及所取得的
     成果。




06   Transformation from Digitalization
     數字化轉型
     Modern Dairy was honored with the “2023 CDI China Digital Enterprise TOP20” award and “2023 Dingge
     Award for Digital Transformation Pioneer List” by the Annual Jury Committee, symbolizing our success in
     digitalization transformation.
     現代牧業榮獲「2023CDI中國數字化企業TOP20」及「2023 • 鼎革獎數字化轉型先鋒榜」年度評委會大獎，標
     誌着我們在數字化轉型的成功。




07   ESG Achievement
     ESG獎項
     Modern Dairy was honored with the “2023 Netease New Energy Annual ESG Pioneer Enterprise Award”,
     and was listed on the “2023 Bloomberg Green Finance ESG50 Annual Environmental Rankings”. Our
     pioneering practice in resources utilization was selected for inclusion in the COP28 “2023 Corporate
     Climate Action Case Studies”.
     現代牧業榮獲2023年網易新能量「年度ESG先鋒企業獎」大獎及榮登「2023彭博綠金ESG50評選年度環境榜
     單」。我們的資源化利用先鋒實踐入選COP28《2023企業氣候行動案例集》。




08   “Modern No. 1” Genomic Liquid Phase “Chip”
     自主研發「現代1號」基因組液相「芯片」
     Meng Yuan Genetics independently developed “Modern No. 1” Genomic Liquid Phase “Chip”, filling
     in the domestic commercial dairy cows genomic “chip” market. We have completed the preliminary
     construction of the milk production trait prediction model, which is the first genomic reference
     population model in China that can be used for predicting dairy cow-related traits.
     蒙元種業自主研發「現代1號」基因組液相「芯片」填補國內商業化奶牛基因組」芯片」的空白。我們完成了
     產奶性狀預測模型的初步構建，該預測模型為國內首個可用於奶牛產奶相關性狀預測的基因組參考群模
     型。

 MAJOR AWARDS AND ACHIEVEMENTS
 主要獎項及殊榮




     09       Employer Branding Award for DEI
              DEI僱主品牌大獎
              In December 2023, Modern Dairy was honored with the “2023 Employer Branding Award for DEI (Diversity,
              Equity, and Inclusion)”. We place great importance on the management philosophy of corporate
              diversity, equity, and inclusion (DEI). Building a DEI culture within the Group helps enhance personnel
              management capabilities and strengthens the Company’s brand development.
              2023年12月，現代牧業榮膺「2023年度DEI僱主品牌大獎」，我們非常重視企業多元（Diversity）、公平
             （Equity）及包容（Inclusion）文化的管理理念。企業DEI建設有助企提升人員管理水平與能力，强化公司的
              品牌建設。




     10       Team Building with the Spirit of the Gobi
              築夢戈壁魂 • 聚力贏未來
              In October 2023, more than 200 core members of our Group, along with the talents of the Mengniu milk
              source division, gathered in Dunhuang with the belief of “Born to be Strong”. We embarked on the
              challenging journey of the Xuanzang’s Road – Gobi Challenge. The race lasted for 3 days and 2 nights,
              covering a total distance of 88 km. It was not only an extreme challenge but also a remarkable feat.
              Facing the interwoven sand dunes, relentless winds and sands, and the boundless expanse of the desert,
              we overcame each obstacle one by one. The Gobi Challenge not only allowed us to embody the culture
              of resilience, but also strengthened our unity, power and resilience.
              2023年10月，本集團二百多名中堅連同蒙牛奶源事業部骨幹齊聚敦煌，大家懷著「天生要強」的信念，深
              入戈壁，成功完成玄奘之路 – 戈壁挑戰賽。賽程三日兩夜，全程88公里，這是一場極限挑戰也是個壯
              舉。面對著縱橫交錯的沙丘，無情的風沙，浩瀚無涯的大漠，我們也一一克服了。戈壁挑戰賽不僅讓我
              們踐行了要強的文化，更讓我們更團結，更有力量，更加強大。

 of the risk management and internal control system of the Group.        系統之有效性。檢討範圍由審核委員會決定及

 the Audit Committee. The review covers all material controls,           監控、運作監控、合規監控及風險管理職能。該

      evaluate the risk portfolio accordingly.                               估風險組合。

 •    Management: Consider the risk responses, ensure effective          •   管理：考慮風險回應措施，確保與董事會有
      communication to the Board and on-going monitoring of the              效溝通並持續監控剩餘風險。
      residual risks.

 RELATIONSHIPS WITH CUSTOMERS                                           與客戶的關係
 To provide high quality products and services to customers are         為客戶提供高品質的產品和服務是我們的使命，
 always our mission. Customers’ need and demand are always              客戶的需要和需求始終是我們的首要考慮因素。
 our top consideration. With the increase of income and the             隨著我國居民收入水準的提高和健康觀念的普
 popularization of health concepts, the demand of dairy products        及，奶製品需求量日益增大，需求形式的多樣性
 is increasingly augmented with a growing tendency for diversified      也更加明顯。
 products.

 We, together with Mengniu Group expand the sales network and           我們與蒙牛集團也拓寬銷售網絡、豐富銷售形
 increase sale forms, setting up outlets in large supermarkets. By      式，於大型超市參與商超促銷。我們希望能藉此

 (a)   The purpose of the Share Award Scheme II                      (a)   股份獎勵計劃II的目的
       The purpose of the Share Award Scheme II is to recognise            股份獎勵計劃II旨在表揚及激勵本集團若干
       and motivate the contribution of certain members of                 管理層成員的貢獻，提供獎勵及協助本集團
       management of the Group and to provide incentives and help          挽留其現有管理層成員，並為彼等提供實現
       the Group in retaining its existing members of management           本集團長期業務目標的直接經濟利益。
       and to provide them with a direct economic interest in
       attaining the long-term business objectives of the Group.

 (b)   Eligible Participants                                         (b)   合資格參與者
       The eligible participants include all members of the middle         合資格參與者包括本集團任何成員公司的所
       and senior management of any member of the Group.                   有中高級管理人員。

Operational Risks                                                        營運風險
Operational risk is concerned with possible losses caused by failure     營運風險有關內部程序失效、人員、制度或外部
of internal processes, people, system or external events, such           事項造成的可能損失，此風險透過建立健全的內
risk is mitigated by and controlled by establishing sound internal       部監控、明確責任範圍、適當的職責分離及有效
control, setting out clear lines of responsibility, proper segregation   的內部報告予以降低及控制。管理層定期檢討本
of duties and effective internal reporting. Management regularly         集團營運以確保本集團虧損（不論是財務或其他方
reviews the Group’s operations to ensure that the Group’s risk of        面）、欺詐所導致、錯誤、疏忽或其他營運及合規
losses, whether financial or otherwise, resulting from fraud, errors,    事宜的風險得到充分管理。
omissions and other operational and compliance matters, are
adequately managed.

Financial Risks                                                          財務風險

regular reviews of the Group’s net foreign exchange exposures            貨幣遠期合約或貨幣期權合約（如需要），以管理
and may enter into currency forward contracts or currency option         其外匯風險。此外，為應對利率波動的風險，管
contracts, when necessary, to manage its foreign exchange                理層定期檢討及評估本集團的利率變動風險，並
exposure. Furthermore, with aim to tackle the risk of interest rate      可考慮在必要時訂立利率掉期合約或其他對沖策
fluctuation, the management regularly reviews and assesses the           略。
Group’s exposure to interest rate movement and may consider

strategies when necessary.

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                               1. 一般資料
  China Modern Dairy Holdings Ltd. (the “Company”) was                 中國現代牧業控股有限公司（             「本公司」   ）為一
  incorporated and registered in the Cayman Islands as an              間根據開曼群島公司法在開曼群島註冊成
  exempted company with limited liability under the Companies          立及登記的獲豁免有限公司，其股份已自
  Law of the Cayman Islands. Its shares have been listed on the        2010年11月26日起在香港聯合交易所有限公
  Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the           司（「聯交所」     ）主板上市。本公司的註冊辦事
  “Stock Exchange”) since 26 November 2010. The registered             處位於Maples Corporate Services Limited,
  office of the Company is Maples Corporate Services Limited,          PO Box 309, Ugland House, Grand Cayman,
  PO Box 309, Ugland House, Grand Cayman, KYI-1104,                    KYI-1104, Cayman Islands。
  Cayman Islands.

  As at 31 December 2023, China Mengniu Dairy Co., Ltd.                於2023年12月31日，中國蒙牛乳業有限公司
  (“Mengniu”) and its wholly-owned subsidiary together owned          （
                                                                      「蒙牛」  ）及其全資附屬公司共同擁有本公司
  56.36% of the issued share capital of the Company. Mengniu           已發行股本的56.36%。蒙牛及其附屬公司
  and its subsidiaries are hereinafter collectively referred to as     於下文統稱「蒙牛集團」。
  “Mengniu Group”.

  The principal activity of the Company is investment holding          本公司的主要業務為投資控股，而其附屬公
  and its subsidiaries are mainly engaged in production and            司則主要從事生產及銷售牛奶、買賣、生產
  sales of milk, trading, production and sales of feeds. The           及銷售飼料。本公司及其附屬公司於下文統
  Company and its subsidiaries are hereinafter collectively            稱「本集團」。
  referred to as the “Group”.

  The consolidated financial statements are presented in               綜合財務報表以人民幣（
                                                                                 「人民幣」
                                                                                     ）列值，該

   3.2 Material accounting policy information                          3.2 重大會計政策資料
       Basis of consolidation                                              綜合基準
       The consolidated financial statements incorporate                   綜合財務報表載有本公司以及由本公
       the financial statements of the Company and entities                司及其附屬公司控制的實體（包括結構
       (including structured entities) controlled by the                   性實體）的財務報表。倘屬以下情況，
       Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when              則本公司獲得控制權：
       the Company:

       •    has power over the investee;                                   •   可對被投資者行使權力；

       •    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from            •   因參與被投資者的業務而可獲得
            its involvement with the investee; and                             或有權獲得可變回報；及

       •    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.        •   有能力藉行使其權力而影響該等
                                                                               回報。

       The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                  倘有事實或情況顯示上述三項控制因
       investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there             素中，有一項或以上出現變數，本集
       are changes to one or more of the three elements of                 團會重新評估其是否控制被投資者。
       control listed above.

       Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group                 本集團於獲得附屬公司控制權時將附
       obtains control over the subsidiary and ceases when                 屬公司綜合入賬，並於失去附屬公司
       the Group loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically,            控制權時終止入賬。具體而言，於年
       income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or                     內購入或出售的附屬公司的收入及開
       disposed of during the year are included in the                     支，自本集團獲得控制權當日起至本
       consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                  集團失去附屬公司控制權當日止，計
       comprehensive income from the date the Group gains                  入綜合損益及其他全面收益表內。
       control until the date when the Group ceases to control
       the subsidiary.

       Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive                 損益及各項其他全面收入項目歸屬於
       income are attributed to the owners of the                          本公司擁有人及非控股權益。附屬公
       Company and to the non-controlling interests. Total                 司的全面收入總額歸屬於本公司擁有
       comprehensive income of subsidiaries is attributed to               人及非控股權益，即使這將導致非控
       the owners of the Company and to the non-controlling                股權益出現虧絀結餘。
       interests even if this results in the non-controlling
       interests having a deficit balance.

       When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial               如有需要，將會就附屬公司的財務報
       statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting                表作出調整，使其會計政策與本集團
       policies in line with the Group’s accounting policies.              的會計政策貫徹一致。

            non-controlling interests’ proportionate interests.

            Any difference between the amount by which the non-                            非控股權益調整金額與支付或收到代
            controlling interests are adjusted, and the fair value of                      價的公平值之間的任何差額均直接於

        substantive if they are critical to the ability to continue        要，包括具備執行相關過程所必需的

        with the necessary skills, knowledge, or experience                或對持續生產產出的能力有重大貢

        and are considered unique or scarce or cannot be                   持續生產產出能力出現延遲的情況下
        replaced without significant cost, effort, or delay in the         不可取代。
        ability to continue producing outputs.

        Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the             業務收購乃採用收購法入賬。業務合

        acquiree and the equity interests issued by the Group              和。收購相關成本一般於產生時在損
        in exchange for control of the acquiree. Acquisition-              益中確認。
        related costs are generally recognised in profit or loss

        After the commencement date, lease liabilities are                 在開始日期後，租賃負債透過利息增

        group entity, transactions in currencies other than the            以 該 實 體 的 功 能 貨 幣 以 外 的 貨 幣（外

            Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical              信貸虧損。評估乃根據本集團的過往

            the reporting date as well as the forecast of future              作出調整。
            conditions.

       (v)  Measurement and recognition of ECL                           (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
            The measurement of ECL is a function of the                      預期信貸虧損的計量取決於違約
            probability of default, loss given default (i.e.                 概率、違約虧損率（即違約時的
            the magnitude of the loss if there is a default)                 虧損程度）及違約風險承擔。違
            and the exposure at default. The assessment                      約概率及違約虧損率的評估乃基
            of the probability of default and loss given                     於過往數據及前瞻性資料作出。
            default is based on historical data and forward-                 預期信貸虧損的預估反映無偏
            looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects                  概率加權金額，以各自發生違約
            an unbiased and probability-weighted amount                      的風險為權重釐定。本集團在考
            that is determined with the respective risks of                  慮過往信貸虧損經驗及無需過度
            default occurring as the weights. The Group uses                 成本或努力即可獲得的前瞻性資
            a practical expedient in estimating ECL on trade                 料的情況下，使用撥備矩陣估計
            receivables of feeds using a provision matrix                    飼料應收貿易賬款的預期信貸虧
            taking into consideration historical credit loss                 損。
            experience and forward looking information that
            is available without undue cost or effort.

             Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據
             contractual cash flows that are due to the Group                合約應付本集團的所有合約現金
             in accordance with the contract and the cash flows              流量與本集團預期收取的現金流
             that the Group expects to receive, discounted                   量（按於初始確認時釐定的實際
             at the effective interest rate determined at initial            利率貼現）之間的差額。該等資
             recognition. The ECL on these assets are assessed               產的預期信貸虧損單獨或通過適
             individually or collectively with appropriate                   當分組集體評估。
             groupings.

   UNCERTAINTY
   In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which         於 應 用 本 集 團 的 會 計 政 策（於 附 註 3闡 述）
   are described in Note 3, the Directors are required to               時，董事須就資產及負債不易由其他來源得
   make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the                 出的賬面值作出判斷、估計及假設。估計及
   carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily      有關假設乃基於過往經驗和被視為屬相關的
   apparent from other sources. The estimates and associated            其他因素作出。實際結果可能與該等估計有
   assumptions are based on historical experience and other             所不同。
   factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual results may
   differ from these estimates.

   The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on             估計及相關假設會持續進行檢討。倘對會計
   an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are             估計的修訂僅影響對估計作出修訂的期間，
   recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if         則於修訂有關估計的期間內確認，或倘修訂
   the revision affects only that period, or in the period of the       對當前及未來期間均有影響，則於修訂期間
   revision and future periods if the revision affects both current     及未來期間確認。
   and future periods.

  IFRS 8 Operating Segments requires operating segments             國際財務報告準則第8號經營分部 規定根據
  to be identified on the basis of internal reports about           主要營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」  ）定期審
  components of the Group that are regularly reviewed by the        閱的有關本集團組成部分的內部報告識別經
  chief operating decision maker (the “CODM”), in order to          營分部，以分配資源及評估表現。自2022年
  allocate resources and assess performance. From July 2022,        7月起，本集團於2022年6月28日收購內蒙古
  the Group commenced the business of digital intelligence          愛養牛科技有限公司（ 「愛養牛」 ）後開始數智
  platform after acquisition of Inner Mongolia Ai Yang Niu          平台業務，於本年度被主要營運決策者視為
  Technology Co., Ltd. (“Aiyangniu”) on 28 June 2022, which         新的呈報分部。上一年度分部披露已作重列
  was considered as a new reportable segment by the CODM            以與本年度的呈列一致。為分配資源及評估
  during the current year. Prior year segment disclosures           分部表現而向主要營運決策者報告的資料集
  have been represented to conform with the current year’s          中於所交付貨物及服務的類型及渠道。
  presentation. Information reported to the CODM for the
  purposes of resources allocation and assessment of segment
  performance focuses on the types and channels of goods and
  services delivered.

  The Group’s reportable segments under IFRS 8 are as follows:      本集團於國際財務報告準則第8號下的呈報

 11. INCOME TAX EXPENSE AND DEFERRED                                11. 所得稅開支及遞延稅項

 22. BIOLOGICAL ASSETS                                                  22. 生物資產
      A – Nature of activities                                            A – 業務性質
      The biological assets of the Group are dairy cows held to           本集團的生物資產為持有作生產原料奶的乳
      produce raw milk.                                                   牛。

      The quantity of dairy cows owned by the Group at end of the         以下是本集團於報告期末擁有的乳牛數量。

    The Group is exposed to fair value risks arising from changes       本集團要承受原料奶價格變動帶來的公平值
    in price of raw milk. The Group does not anticipate that            風險。據本集團預計，在可預見的將來原料
    the price of the raw milk will decline significantly in the         奶價格不會大幅下跌。董事認為，本集團並
    foreseeable future and the Directors are of the view that there     無可用的衍生工具或其他可以訂立的合約用
    is no available derivative or other contracts which the Group       於管理原料奶價格下跌的風險。
    can enter into to manage the risk of a decline in the price of
    the raw milk.

    In general, the heifers are inseminated with semen when             一般情況下，小母牛於長至約十四個月大時
    heifers reached approximately 14 months old. After an               進行配種。約九個月的孕期過後，小牛出
    approximately nine months’ pregnancy term, a calf is born           生，奶牛開始生產原料奶及哺乳期開始。奶
    and the dairy cow begins to produce raw milk and the                牛通常會擠奶約340天，之後進入約60天的
    lactation period begins. A milkable cow is typically milked for     乾乳期。
    approximately 340 days before a dry period of approximately
    60 days.

    When a heifer begins to produce raw milk, it is transferred         當小母牛開始產原料奶時，乃按轉撥日期的
    to the category of milkable cows based on the estimated fair        估計公平值轉撥至奶牛類別。
    value on the date of transfer.

    The Group is exposed to a number of risks related to its            本集團面臨與其生物資產有關的多種風險。
    biological assets. The Group is exposed to the following            本集團面臨以下運營風險：
    operating risks:

    i.    Regulatory and environmental risks                            i.    法規及環境風險
          The Group is subject to laws and regulations in the                 本集團須遵守其開展農場種植及育種
          location in which it operates plantation and breeding.              活動所在地的法律及法規。本集團已
          The Group has established environmental policies                    制定旨在遵守當地環境及其他法律的
          and procedures aimed at compliance with local                       環境政策及程序。管理層進行定期檢
          environmental and other laws. Management performs                   查以識別環境風險及確保現有系統足
          regular reviews to identify environmental risks and                 以管理該等風險。
          to ensure that the systems in place are adequate to
          manage these risks.

    ii.   Climate, disease and other natural risks                      ii.   氣候、疾病及其他自然風險
          The Group’s biological assets are exposed to the risk               本集團的生物資產面臨遭受氣候變
          of damage from climatic changes, diseases and other                 化、疾病及其他自然因素損害的風
          natural forces. The Group has extensive processes in                險。本集團已擴展監察及減輕該等風
          place aimed at monitoring and mitigating those risks,               險的程序，包括定期檢查及疾病控制
          including regular inspections and disease controls and              與調查及保險。
          surveys and insurance.

   The PRC employees of the Group are eligible members of            本集團的中國僱員為中國政府營辦的國家管
   the state-managed retirement benefit schemes operated             理退休福利計劃的合資格成員。本公司於中
   by the government of the PRC. The employees of the PRC            國的附屬公司的員工須按員工養老保險的要
   subsidiaries of the company are required to contribute in         求每月向國家管理退休福利計劃作出供款以
   accordance with employee pension insurance requirements           撥付有關福利。本集團於國家管理退休福利
   on monthly basis to the state-managed retirement benefit          計劃的唯一責任為根據有關計劃作出規定的
   schemes to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the          供款。
   Group with respect to the state-managed retirement benefit
   schemes is to make the required contributions under the
   schemes.

   Other subsidiaries in jurisdictions outside of the PRC            中國以外司法權區的其他附屬公司代表其僱
   contribute to statutory retirement plans on behalf of their       員向法定退休計劃供款，該等計劃在許多情
   employees which in many cases are managed by the                  況下由有關司法權區的政府管理。該等附屬
   government in those jurisdictions. Those subsidiaries make        公司按規定定期向法定退休計劃供款，供款
   periodic contributions to the statutory retirement plans as       一般按僱員基本薪金的百分比或每名僱員每
   required and contributions are generally determined as a          個曆年的固定金額（視乎司法權區的法律及
   percentage of employees’ basic salaries or a fixed amount         法規而定）釐定。
   per employee per calendar year depending on laws and
   regulations of the jurisdiction.

   At 31 December 2023 and 2022, the Group had no forfeited          於2023年及2022年12月31日，本集團並無
   contributions under the above retirement benefit schemes          上述退休福利計劃項下被沒收的供款，本集
   which may be used by the Group to reduce the existing level       團可能會使用該等供款來降低現有供款水
   of contributions. There were also no forfeited contributions      平。根據該計劃，於2023年及2022年12月
   available at 31 December 2023 and 2022 under such schemes         31日亦無可供本集團用於減少未來年度應付
   which may be used by the Group to reduce the contribution         供款的被沒收供款。
   payable in future years.

 40. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                              40. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in              本集團的資金管理旨在確保本集團旗下實體
      the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                 的持續經營能力，同時透過優化債務及股本
      while maximising the return to stakeholders through the               結餘為利益相關者謀求最大回報。本集團的
      optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s              整體策略較去年保持不變。
      overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.

      The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which        本集團的資本架構由債務淨額（主要包括銀
      mainly includes bank borrowings, other borrowings, short              行借款、其他借款、短期融資券、長期債券
      term debenture, long term bonds and lease liabilities as              及租賃負債（分別披露於附註28、29、30、
      disclosed in Notes 28, 29, 30, 31 and 32 respectively, net of         31及32），扣除銀行結餘及現金）及本公司擁
      bank balances and cash and equity attributable to owners              有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢價
      of the Company, comprising issued share capital, share                及儲備）組成。
      premium and reserves.

      The Group’s management reviews the capital structure                  本集團的管理層定期檢討資本架構。作為檢
      on a regular basis. As part of the review, the management             討的一環，管理層會考慮資本成本及各類資
      considers the cost of capital and the risks associated with           本涉及的風險。本集團透過發行新股份及發
      each class of capital. The Group will balance its overall capital     行新債務或贖回現有債務等方式保持整體資
      structure through the issues of new shares and the issue of           本架構的平衡。
      new debt or the redemption of existing debt.

44. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS                                             44. 公平值計量
   Fair value of the Group’s biological assets, financial                 按經常性基準以公平值減銷售成本或公
   assets and financial liabilities that are measured at                  平值計量的本集團生物資產、金融資產
   fair value less costs to sell or fair value on a recurring             及金融負債的公平值
   basis
   The Group’s biological assets are measured at fair value less          本集團的生物資產按公平值減出售成本計
   costs to sell, certain financial instruments are measured at           量，若干金融工具於各報告期末按公平值計
   fair values at the end of each reporting period. The Directors         量。董事已成立估值團隊，由本公司首席財
   have set up a valuation team, which is headed up by the                務官領導，以釐定適當的估值技術及公平值
   chief financial officer of the Company, to determine the               計量的輸入數據。
   appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value
   measurements.

   In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group     估計資產或負債的公平值時，本集團使用可
   uses market observable data to the extent it is available.             用市場可觀察數據。倘第一級及第二級的輸
   Where Level 1 and Level 2 inputs are not available, the Group          入數據不可用，本集團會委聘第三方合資格
   engages third party qualified valuers to perform the valuation.        估值師進行估值。估值團隊與合資格外聘估
   The valuation team works closely with the qualified external           值師緊密合作，為模型確立合適的估值方法
   valuers to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and          及輸入數據。首席財務官每半年向董事呈報
   inputs to the model. The chief financial officer reports the           估值團隊的發現以闡釋資產及負債公平值波
   valuation team’s findings to the Directors semi-annually to            動的原因。
   explain the cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets
   and liabilities.

                               Garman-Kohlhagen模型及貼現現金流量
                               Since it is fixed-to-fixed cross currency
                                 swap, future cash flows are estimated
                                 based on the contract. Different cash
                                 flows in different currencies are
                                 discounted via the corresponding curves,
                                 and netted into the same currency as at
                                 the valuation date.
                               由於是固定至固定的跨貨幣掉期，
                                 未來現金流量乃根據合約估計。
                                 不同貨幣的不同現金流量通過相應的
                                 曲線貼現，並在估值日以相同的
                                 貨幣淨額計算。
